- Fast functions:

For convolution c_r = sum_{i+j=r} a_i a_j. Derivative: ∂c_r/∂a_i = a_{r-i} if valid, plus if both same (2i=r) then derivative is 2a_i? Let's compute with c_r = sum_i a_i a_{r-i}; When differentiating w.r.t a_i, terms: i fixed first factor a_i * a_{r-i}; also term j=i in sum? Actually the sum over all pairs i from 0..n-1, j=r-i. If i and j distinct, product appears once in sum (not twice) because the sum is over i only, not ordered pairs. For c_r = ∑_{i} a_i a_{r-i}. Derivative = a_{r-i} (from term with i) + ∑ over terms where r-i = i? Let's do small: c_1=a0a1+a1a0? For n=2 conv [a0^2,2a0a1,a1^2]. Wait np.convolve includes 2a0a1 because sum_i a_i a_{1-i}: i=0 a0a1, i=1 a1a0 same product but in formula counted twice? Yes because i ranges all, and both i and r-i appear separately if distinct. So c_r = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1} a_i a_{r-i} (zero if out of range). For distinct i,j, both terms a_i a_j and a_j a_i are included, giving 2a_i a_j. Pseudo:

```
import numpy as np, time
try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    HAVE_SCIPY=True
except: HAVE_SCIPY=False

def fft_conv(a): ...
def fast_score(a): ...
def normalize(a): ...
def official_score(seq): ...

def smooth_max_weights(vals, beta): ...
def corr_weights(a, w): ...
def smooth_grad_p(p, beta): ...
def smooth_grad_theta(theta, beta, deadline=None): ...

def softmax(theta): ...
def theta_from_p(p): ...

def lbfqgs_smooth(theta0, beta, bounds, maxiter, deadline, ftol...):
    if not HAVE_SCIPY: return theta0
    args=(beta, deadline)
    options...
    res=minimize(smooth_theta_objective, theta0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options=options, args=args)
    return np.asarray(res.x)
```

But `minimize` with args passes to fun/jac. Our fun returns tuple if jac=True; args include beta, deadline.
```
def smooth_theta_objective(theta, beta, deadline=0.0):
    if time.monotonic() > deadline: return inf, zero
    a=softmax(theta)
    b=fft_conv(a)
    G,w=smooth_max(b,beta)
    ga=2*corr_weights(a,w)
    gt=a*ga - a*np.dot(a,ga)
    if not finite: return inf, zero
    return G, gt
```
Need top-level zero? Use `np.zeros_like(theta)`.

L-BFGS with `maxiter` and time: If fun returns inf at deadline, L-BFGS may error? It will likely stop. We can catch.

However high dimension L-BFGS with bounds and objective G not scaled? G ~0.125? Maybe okay.

- Since softmax has natural scale, gradient relative. Use gradient descent on theta with line search? We can do simple backtracking on theta: for trial sizes, q=theta - lr*grad, clip bounds, convert to p, exact score. Accept if exact score improves (or smooth). This is similar to gradient but in log space, no projection. It might be more robust than simplex projection because zero values not problematic. We can implement our own loop, maybe better than L-BFGS.

Let's design a high-level search:

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    reserve=90
    deadline=start+budget-reserve
    # choose N: 8192 maybe if budget>1000 else 4096
    N=8192
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    candidates=[]
    # official test of height_sequence_1
    try: hs=height_sequence_1; if list len>0: candidates.append(normalize positive clipped)
    except: pass
    candidates += parametric_starts(N,rng)
    # choose top 5 by fast_score
    best_p,best_score = top[0],score
    # optional: if best score not finite, uniform
    # Run refinement pipelines on top starts with time allocations
    for idx,p in enumerate(top_starts):
        remain = deadline - now - reserve_final
        if remain<60: break
        # allocate fraction based on score
        # Pipeline:
        # 1. Block coarse-to-fine from p (maybe bounded by remaining*0.3)
        # 2. Full block L-BFGS (remaining*0.5)
        # 3. Projected gradient final (remaining*0.2)
        p,sc = refine_start(p, deadline_start...)
        update best
    # final official, return if finite and <=2, else uniform
```

Need not overcomplicate. Since time large, we can do a focused refinement on the best previous start:
- If `height_sequence_1` exists and has good score, use it as primary.
- Run block optimization with B up to maybe 2048 from it and from parametric starts.
- Run full softmax/block L-BFGS beta stages from best.
- Final Adam/polish.

Block start c from p and orientation:
```
def c_from_block(p,n,B):
   L=n//B
   bs = p.reshape(B,L).sum(axis=1)
   bs = np.maximum(bs,1e-300)
   ref = bs[-1]
   c = np.log(bs[:-1]/ref)
   return np.clip(c, -20,20)
```
If B=n, L=1, bs=p.

```

Need generate smooth random direction in log space to avoid killing structure. Use Fourier:
```
def smooth_rand_multivariate(n,rng,sigma=0.2):
   z = rng.standard_normal(n)
   # lowpass: multiply FFT by exp(-freq^2/width)
   Z = np.fft.rfft(z)
   freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
   Z *= np.exp(-(freq*n/10)**2)
   d = np.fft.irfft(Z,n)
   d = (d - d.mean()) / max(np.std(d),1e-12)
   return d
```
But no closures; top-level.

Apply p = normalize(np.maximum(p*(1+σ*d), 1e-12)). Or theta=log(p)+σ*d.

Phase 1: Candidate generation:
- Include `height_sequence_1` if exists (and reversed).
- Generate a pool of parametric and random block starts.
- For each, compute fast_score.
- Select top `num_starts=4` (ensure diversity: maybe not all similar? Could use simple: top scores; if reversed duplicates okay).
- best_p, best_score.

Phase 2: Coarse-to-fine block optimization on each top start (budget 35% maybe).
For each start with allocated fraction (based on rank):
  p = start
  for B in [8,16,32,64,128,256,512,1024,2048] up to N/4:
    orient p
    c = c_from_block
    run block L-BFGS beta=5000, maxiter maybe 200, bounds [-12,12]
    if improved run beta=30000, maxiter 200
    update p
  end
  global best update
Stop when time.

Because block objective uses n=8192 even for small B, cost per fun independent of B mostly. But L-BFGS with small B cheap.

Phase 3: Full refinement on global best (remaining ~50%):
- Use full block L-BFGS B=N with beta stages. This can further improve.
- Also maybe use direct gradient polish.

Phase 4: Escaping/perturbation (remaining): short full block from perturbations of best.

Parametric starts details to cover possibilities:
```
def make_parametric_starts(n, rng):
  starts=[]
  x = (np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
  # uniform
  starts.append(np.full(n,1/n))
  # symmetric cosh with varying sharpness:
  for lam in [0.5,1,2,3,5,8]:
     z = lam * (2*x-1)  # high ends
     p = np.cosh(z)
     starts.append(normalize(p))
  # power law high ends:
  for alpha in [0.2,0.5,0.8,1.2]:
     y = np.abs(2*x-1)
     p = y**(-alpha)  # high at ends? y=1 ends? Wait y=abs(2x-1): 0 at center,1 ends. y^-alpha high ends, singular at center! We want high ends, low center: use (1-y)^-alpha? 1-y=0 ends singular? Actually want low center: p=(1-y)^-alpha with 1-y=0 at center? If y=0 center ->0^-? high; no. Use y^alpha? high ends, low center: p=y^alpha for alpha>0 gives 0 center. But endpoints finite; for alpha negative high center. We'll use p=np.power(y, alpha) for alpha in [0.3,0.7,1,2]. For alpha small center not too low.
  # exponentials two ends:
  for r in [0.3,0.45,0.6,0.7,0.85]:
     p = np.exp(-r*np.arange(n)) + np.exp(-r*np.arange(n-1,-1,-1))
     starts.append(normalize(p))
  # sinusoidal modulations:
  for k in [1,2,3,4,6,8]:
     for sig in [0.5,1.0]:
        phase = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
        p = np.exp(sig*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x+phase))
        starts.append(normalize(p))
  # random low-pass theta:
  for _ in range(8):
     theta = smooth_random(n,rng,sigma=1.0)
     p=softmax_unnorm(theta)
  return starts
```

def improve_sequence(p0, end_time, tag, budget_frac=1.0, do_full=True):
    p=normalize(p0); sc=fast_score(p)
    # Phase block: up to B_list maybe chosen by expected time.
    B_list = [16,32,64,128,256,512,1024,2048]
    # time per B maybe allocate proportional log? We'll just iterate while time.
    for B in B_list:
        if B>=N or time>end-120: break
        # orient
        q=orient_for_block(p)
        c=c_from_block(q,N,B)
        # low beta
        c=run_block_minimize(c,N,B,5000,bounds, min(end, now+block_time_low), maxiter=150)
        q2=block_to_p(...); sc2=...
        if improved: q=q2; sc=...
        # high beta if time
        if time<end-60:
            c=...; c=run_block_minimize(c,N,B,30000,... maxiter=150)
            q2...
        p=normalize(q)
    # Full if do_full and time > 300:
       full_betases...
    # Polish
    p=polish(p,end_time)
    return p,sc
```

Detailed code:

```
import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY = False
```

Helper time:
```
def now(): return time.monotonic()
```
But `time` built-in? Fine.

`fft_conv(a)` as previous.

`fast_score_array(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
S=a.sum()
if S<=1e-12: return inf
b=fft_conv(a)
if b.size==0: return inf
M=b.max()
if not finite: return inf
return float(2*a.size*M/(S*S))
```
If a length 0 inf.

`normalize_simplex(a)`:
```
a=...; a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s>0 finite: a=s? else uniform
```

`block_to_p(n,B,z)`:
- if B<=0 return empty.
- L=n//B. If L==0? B>n. We'll call B<=n.
- z = np.empty(B); if B>1 set from c; z[-1]=0.
- if not finite return uniform block.
- zmax=np.max(z); e=np.exp(z-zmax); wb=e/e.sum(); if fail uniform.
- p=np.repeat(wb / float(L), L)
- return p
`block_to_p_from_c` maybe.

`c_from_blocks(p,n,B)`:
```
L=n//B
bs=p.reshape(B,L).sum(axis=1)
bs=np.maximum(bs,1e-300)
c=np.log(bs[:-1]/bs[-1])
return np.clip(c, -22,22)
```

`smooth_max_weights(vals,beta)`:
```
vals=np.asarray(vals)
if vals.size==0: return inf, empty
m=float(np.max(vals))
if not finite: return inf, zeros
if m <=0 or beta <=0: w=np.full(vals.size,1/size); return m,w
x=beta*(vals-m); x=np.clip(x,-745,0)

```

`corr_weights(a,w)` as previous. Ensure a,w float. For n large, size = nextpow2(n+m-1). Use `np.fft.rfft`.
```
def corr_weights(a,w):
 n=a.size; m=w.size
 if n==0 or m==0: return zeros
 need=n+m-1
 size=1; while size<need: size<<=1
 P=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
 WR=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1].copy(), size)
 C=np.fft.irfft(P*WR,size)[:need]
 s=C[m-n:m][::-1].copy()
 return np.maximum(s,0?)
```
Should we clip negative? In gradient, s can be small negative due FFT; smoothing weights positive, a positive, exact s≥0. Clip to 0 to avoid negative gradient artifacts? Could bias but okay. Use `np.where(s>0,s,0)`.

`block_objective(c, n, B, beta, deadline)`:
```
if now()>deadline: return inf, empty
z=np.empty(B)
if B>1:
  c=np.asarray(c,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
  tmp=np.zeros(B-1); m=min(tmp.size,c.size); tmp[:m]=c[:m]; z[:-1]=tmp
z[-1]=0
if not all finite: return inf, zeros
zmax=np.max(z); e=np.exp(z-zmax); s=e.sum(); if s<=0 or not finite: wb=ones/B; else wb=e/s
L=n//B
p=np.repeat(wb/L, L)
b=fft_conv(p); if b.size==0: inf
G,w=smooth_max_weights(b,beta)
if not finite G: return inf, zeros
svec=corr_weights(p,w)
g=2.0*svec
if L>1: blockgrad = g.reshape(B,L).sum(axis=1)
else: blockgrad = g
dot = float(np.dot(wb, blockgrad))
pref = wb / float(L) if L>1 else wb.copy()
grad_full = pref * (blockgrad - dot)
if B>1:
  grad=grad_full[:-1]
else: grad=np.empty(0)
grad=np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
return float(G), grad.astype(np.float64)
```
Check L=1: pref=wb, blockgrad=g, grad_full=wb*(g-dot). Good.
But if L>1, g.reshape(B,L) assumes p length n and B blocks of length L. Yes.

However for smooth objective, value G. Should we multiply by `n` to improve line search? The scale of G ~1/n=1e-4; gradient ~1/n? L-BFGS may have small numbers but okay. Multiplying objective by n gives O(1) and gradient O(1). `minimize` ftol relative maybe better. Use value `n*G` and gradient `n*grad_full[:-1]`. This doesn't change optimum. Let's do that to improve line search. In block_objective return `float(n*G)`, grad `n*grad`? Wait previous used n*G. Yes. Then `G` for comparison? exact score uses 2nM/(S^2). `smooth_random_theta(n, rng, sigma=1.0, cor=8.0)` top-level:
```

freq=np.fft.rfftfreq(n)

if d.__len__()!=n: d=d[:]
d -= d.mean()
std=d.std()
if std<1e-12: d=rng.standard_normal(n)
return sigma*d/std
```

Top function:
```
def select_top(candidate_pairs, count):
  scored=[]
  for idx,p in enumerate(candidate_pairs):
    sc=fast_score_array(p)
    if np.isfinite(sc): scored.append((sc,idx))
  scored.sort()
  top=[]
  for sc,idx in scored[:count]: top.append(candidate_pairs[idx])
  return top
```

def improve_sequence(p0, end_time, do_full=True):
    p=normalize(p0); sc=fast_score
    # Block
    block_end = min(end_time, now()+ max(60, 0.5*(end_time-now)))
    B_list=[16,32,64,128,256,512,1024,2048,4096]
    for B in B_list:
       if B> N and B<N: maybe use B divisors? Use global N? But function doesn't know N? Pass n.
       n=p.size
       if B>=n or now()>block_end-60: break
       if n % B !=0: continue
       q=orient(q)
       c=c_from_block(q,n,B)
       sub_end = min(block_end, now()+90)
       c=run_block_minimize(c,n,B,5000,sub_end,150)
       q2=block_to_p(n,B,c); sc2=fast_score(q2)
       if finite and sc2<sc: q=q2; sc=sc2
       if now() < block_end-45:
          c=c_from_block(q,n,B)
          sub_end=min(block_end, now()+90)
          c=run_block_minimize(c,n,B,30000,sub_end,150)
          q2=...; if sc2<sc update
       p=q
    # Full
    if do_full and now() < end_time - 240:
       n=p.size
       stages=[(5000,300),(20000,400),(80000,300)]
       for idx,(beta,maxit) in enumerate(stages):
          if now() > end_time - 120: break
          q=orient(p)
          c=c_from_block(q,n,n)
          sub_end = min(end_time - 120, now() + [300,350,300][idx])
          if sub_end <= now()+30: continue
          c=run_block_minimize(c,n,n,beta,sub_end,maxit)
          q2=block_to_p(n,n,c)
          sc2=fast_score(q2)
          if sc2<sc: q=q2; sc=sc2
          p=q
    # final short polish high beta
    if now() < end_time - 30:
       q=orient(p); c=...; sub_end=end_time-10; c=run_block_minimize(..., beta=150000, maxit=200)
       ...
    return normalize(p), score
```

Let's ponder mathematical structure more to find better deterministic construction.

We want minimize max q_r of A(z)^2. Since coefficients nonnegative. This is equivalent to selecting a probability distribution p on {0,...,n-1} minimizing the maximum probability of X+Y where X,Y independent with distribution p. This is a combinatorial additive combinatorics problem. For continuous distributions on [0,1], minimize sup convolution. Known as "Narrow sets"? If p is continuous density, the minimal sup of f*f maybe 1/2? Is there a density with f*f ≤ 3/4? Let's see via Fourier uncertainty. There is a theorem: For nonnegative f with L1=1, ||f*f||∞ ≥ 1/2 maybe. Uniform gives 1; can reduce to 1/2? Maybe yes with density like arcsine? Target score 1.5027 -> continuous max 0.751, slightly above 0.75. Maybe infimum 0.75? There might be explicit density achieving 0.75? If continuous infimum 0.75, target 1.5027 (max 0.75135) close to 0.75. Maybe optimal continuous is known and simple! We need find it.

Let's attempt solve continuous minimax f*f with f≥0 on [0,1]. Since convolution support [0,2]. Symmetric optimum likely f(x)=f(1-x) because if not, symmetrize? If replace f by (f(x)+f(1-x))/2, the convolution? Does max not increase by convexity? f*f is not convex in f? But maybe by rearrangement, symmetric is optimal. Assume symmetric f.

We want max g(t)=f*f(t) constant perhaps at several t. The lower bound maybe 1/2? Let's test simple symmetric densities:
- Uniform: g(t)=t for t∈[0,1], max 1.
- U-shaped f(x) ∝ 1/√{x(1-x)}? Compute g(1)=∫ f(x)f(1-x) dx. If f symmetric singular endpoints: f(x)~C/√x near 0, f(1-x)~C/√(1-x); near x=0, product C^2/√(x(1-x)); integral near 0 diverges! g(1) infinite, bad. So endpoints too singular increase center.
- Dense at endpoints with algebraic not too singular? f(x) ∝ x^{-α}, 0<α<0.5 integrable. g(1) near x=0 ~ x^{-α}(1-x)^{-α}? f symmetric: f(1-x) ~ (1-x)^{-α}? near 0 ~1, product ~x^{-α}, integrable. g(1) finite. Parametrize Fourier coefficients directly: c_0 fixed 1 (mean). Variables θ = [c1s,c1c,c2s,...] multiplying basis. Need ensure a_i >0. We can parameterize p = softmax(theta) where theta has Fourier structure: theta_i = ∑ Fourier modes. This ensures positive and sum normalized. The space is n-dimensional but low-dimensional modes. We can optimize modes with L-BFGS using `smooth_fourier_objective`. This is excellent.

Define for given n, K, variables `u` length 2K (or K amp+phase). Compute theta_i = ∑_{k=1}^K (A_k cos(2π k i/n) + B_k sin(...)). Then p=softmax(theta). Since softmax invariant to mean, no need constrain. Objective as before smooth max of p*p. Gradient wrt u: p = softmax(theta); grad_theta_j = p_j (grad_a_j - dot); chain: dtheta/d u_l = basis_l(i). grad_u_l = sum_i grad_theta_i basis_l_i. This is cheap: need basis matrix K x n? K up to 64, n8192, 524k, fine. We can compute gradient via matrix-vector. This allows low-dimensional L-BFGS with beta stages. It can find global structured sequences.

This is a new approach beyond previous. Let's implement.

Function `fourier_basis(n,K)`: precompute? To avoid allocation each objective, we can compute theta via loops in objective. For L-BFGS many calls, precompute basis columns as array shape (K, n). Top-level function can't maintain global state? We can generate inside and pass as arg? `minimize` args can include basis array. But basis size K*n; for K=64, 524k floats 4MB, okay. Could pass. But top-level objective `fourier_objective(u, n, K, beta, deadline, basis)`. If we pass basis each call, okay.

Simpler: Precompute cos/sin basis columns as list? In objective, compute theta from u:
```
theta = np.zeros(n)
i = np.arange(n)
for k in range(1,K+1):
   ang = 2*np.pi*k*i/n
   theta += u[2*(k-1)]*np.cos(ang) + u[2*(k-1)+1]*np.sin(ang)
```
This loop K per objective; with K=32, 32 trig calls each eval expensive. Better precompute basis once per K/n and pass to objective. The objective then `theta = basis.T @ u` (K x n). For K low, fast. Gradient: `grad_theta` length n; `grad_u = basis @ grad_theta`.

But `minimize` args can hold basis (ndarray). Good.

`fourier_objective(u, n, K, beta, deadline, basis)`:
- if time>deadline return inf, zeros(2K)
- theta = basis.T @ u  # basis shape (K, n)
- p = softmax(theta)
- b=fft_conv(p); G,w=smooth_max
- ga=2*corr_weights(p,w)
- gt = p*ga - p*np.dot(p,ga)
- gu = basis @ gt
- val = n*G
- return val, gu

But wait `ga` is gradient wrt a of G (sum=1). For softmax, gt = p*(ga - dot(a,ga)). Good. Chain derivative of theta via basis. Since p=softmax(theta), dθ/d u = basis. Works.

Need basis columns normalized? If basis scale affects gradient. We can normalize each mode to RMS 1 or not. Use standard cos/sin amplitude; variables are Fourier coefficients with units. Fine. To help optimization, scale variables by 1/sqrt(n)? Not necessary. But if u~1, theta variation O(1). Good.

Bounds for u: To keep p nonnegative and not too extreme, theta range should be bounded, e.g. u coefficients sum amplitude maybe ≤6. K modes sum can be larger. Use bounds [-4,4] for each coefficient? Let's derive `fourier_objective` gradient more carefully. `p=softmax(theta)`, `theta = basis.T @ u`. `grad_p = ga` (gradient of G wrt p). `grad_theta = grad_p - p*sum(grad_p*p)`? Actually ga = ∂G/∂p. d p_j/d θ_i = p_j(δ_ji - p_i). Thus ∂G/∂θ_i = ∑_j ga_j p_j(δ_ji - p_i) = ga_i p_j? Wait index: ∂G/∂θ_i = ∑_j ga_j ∂p_j/∂θ_i = ∑_j ga_j p_j(δ_{ji} - p_i) = ga_i p_i - p_i ∑_j ga_j p_j. So vector = p*(ga - dot(p,ga)). This is what we had: `gt = p * (ga - dot)`. Good.
Then ∂G/∂u_l = ∑_i gt_i ∂θ_i/∂u_l = basis_l dot gt. Good.

In `block_objective`, p = repeat softmax(wb)/L. This is not a simple softmax of c with all vars? It is constrained block constant. We derived gradient. Good.

Need ensure `corr_weights` uses weights w for all conv positions; gradient of G wrt p as `2*s`. Let's test for uniform small n. n=2 p=[0.5,0.5], conv [0.25,0.5,0.25]. Suppose smooth weights uniform (beta small) w=1/3. s_i=sum_j p_j w_{i+j}. w all 1/3, s0=0.5*(1/3+1/3+1/3?) i=0: j=0 w0=1/3, j=1 w1=1/3 ->1/3? Actually p0=0.5,p1=0.5, w length3 uniform: s0=0.5*(w0+w1)=1/3, s1=0.5*(w1+w2)=1/3. grad uniform => tangent zero. Good.

For exact max center only, weights w at center=1. For n=3 p uniform [1/3], center r=2, grad_i=2 p_{2-i}? i=0 p2=1/3, i=1 p1=1/3, i=2 p0=1/3, uniform. Stationary. For nonuniform, gradient moves.

`official_score`:
```
def official_score(seq):
    try:
       val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
    except Exception:
       return float('inf')
    try: val=float(val)
    except: return inf
    if np.isfinite(val): return val
    return inf
```

Resample function:
```
def resample_sequence(seq, n):
   a=np.asarray(seq,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
   m=a.size
   if m==n: return normalize? not here
   if m==0: return zeros
   if m==1: return np.full(n,a[0])
   old=np.arange(m,dtype=np.float64)
   new=np.linspace(0.0,m-1,n,dtype=np.float64)
   return np.interp(new,old,a)
```
Then normalize. If a has negative, normalize clips.

Parametric starts code:
```
def parametric_starts(n, rng):
    starts=[]
    starts.append(np.full(n,1.0/n))
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    t=2*x-1
    for lam in [0.5,1.0,2.0,3.0,5.0]:
       p=np.cosh(lam*t)
       starts.append(normalize_simplex(p))
    for alpha in [0.3,0.7,1.0,2.0]:
       p=np.power(np.abs(t), alpha)
       starts.append(normalize_simplex(p))
    for r in [0.3,0.5,0.7,0.85]:
       i=np.arange(n)
       p=np.exp(-r*i)+np.exp(-r*(n-1-i))
       starts.append(normalize_simplex(p))
    for k in [1,2,3,4,6]:
       for sig in [0.5,1.0,2.0]:
          phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
          p=np.exp(sig*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x+phase))
          starts.append(normalize_simplex(p))
    # sine antisymmetric
    for sig in [0.3,0.6,0.9]:
       p=1.0+sig*np.sin(2*np.pi*x)
       starts.append(normalize_simplex(p))
    # smooth random
    for _ in range(6):
       theta=smooth_random_theta(n,rng,sigma=1.0,cor=16)
       p=softmax_from_theta(theta)
       starts.append(p)
    return starts
```
`normalize_simplex` returns length n.

`random_block_starts(n,rng,B_list, count=2)`:
```
for B in B_list:
  if B>=n or n%B: continue
  for _ in range(count):
    c=rng.normal(0,0.5,B-1)
    # lowpass c: apply smoothing by cum? Use np.fft
    c = lowpass_coeffs(c, n//B?) maybe. Simpler convolve with ones(5)
    c = np.convolve(c, np.ones(3)/3, mode='same')
    p=block_to_p(n,B,c)
    starts.append(p)
```
Need `np.convolve` on small c okay. No lambda.

Now, `smooth_random_theta(n,rng,sigma,cor)`:
```
z=np.fft.rfft(rng.standard_normal(n))
freq=np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
z=z*np.exp(-((freq*n)/cor)**2)
d=np.fft.irfft(z,n)
d=d-d.mean()
std=d.std()
if std<1e-12: d=rng.standard_normal(n); std=d.std()
return sigma*d/std
```

Now, Fourier search:
```
def fourier_search(n, K_list, deadline, rng, current_best_score):
    best_p = np.full(n,1/n); best_score=fast_score
    for K in K_list:
       if now() > deadline - 120: break
       basis=build_fourier_basis(n,K)
       limit = 2.0 if K<=8 else (1.5 if K<=16 else 1.0)
       inits=[]
       inits.append(np.zeros(2*K))
       for amp in [0.3,0.7,1.2]: inits.append(amp*rng.normal(size=2*K))
       if K>1: inits.append(...)
       # maybe up to 4
       level_end = deadline - (n? )
       # distribute
       for init in inits:
          if now() > deadline - 60: break
          u=init
          # beta stages
          for beta,maxit in [(1000,200),(5000,300),(20000,300),(80000,200)]:
             if now() > deadline - 60: break
             # sub deadline: maybe stay within some fraction? Use max(30, 0.25*(deadline-now))? But across inits.
             sub_end = min(deadline, now() + max(30, (deadline - now()) / 4.0))
             u=run_fourier_lbfqgs(u,n,K,basis,beta,sub_end,maxit,limit)
             p=softmax_from_theta(basis.T@u)
             sc=fast_score_array(p)
             if finite and sc<best_score: best...
          # after init, if best improved, maybe break? continue.
    return best_p,best_score
```
This may allocate too much per beta: `(deadline-now)/4` per beta, 4 beta = all remaining, then next init no time. Need divide by total number of (init*beta) or at least by inits*4. Better set per stage budget based on total Fourier budget.

Let `fourier_budget = deadline - now()`. Total planned units = len(K_list)*len(inits)*len(beta_stages). But inits variable. We can compute an approximate `per = fourier_budget / (len(K_levels)*max_inits*len(betas))`. Minimum 10s.
Simpler: For each K, allocate `K_budget = (deadline-now()) / remaining_Ks`. For each init, `init_budget = K_budget / len(inits)`. For each beta, `sub_end = now + min(init_budget/len(beta stages) + 10, init_end)`. Good.

for li,K in enumerate(K_list):

`run_fourier_lbfqgs`:
```
def run_fourier_lbfqgs(u0,n,K,basis,beta,deadline,maxiter,limit):
   if not HAVE_SCIPY or K<=0: return u0
   u0=np.asarray(u0,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
   if u0.size != 2*K: ...
   u0=np.clip(u0,-limit,limit)
   bounds=[(-limit,limit)]*(2*K)
   options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-10,'gtol':1e-4,'maxls':30,'maxcor':8}
   try: res=minimize(fourier_objective,u0,args=(n,K,beta,deadline,basis),method='L-BFGS-B',jac=True,bounds=bounds,options=options)
   return res.x
```
`maxcor` memory for 64 vars trivial.

def propose_candidate(...):
  ...
  rng

  else uniform
  print initial

     fourier_end = now() + min(0.30*(work_end-now()), 700)
     p_f,sc_f = fourier_search(n, [4,8,16,32], fourier_end, rng)
     if sc_f < best_score: best=p_f
     print
  # Block improvement on best (and possibly second top)
  if now() < work_end - 200:
     block_end = now() + min(0.30*(work_end-now()), 700)
     p_b,sc_b = improve_block_only(best_p, block_end, max_B=4096)
     if sc_b<best_score: best=p_b
     # if second top and time left:
     if len(top)>1 and now() < block_end - 100 and top[1] is not best? run improve_block_only(top[1], min(block_end, now+...))
  # Full refinement on best
  if now() < work_end - 150:
     full_end = work_end - 100
     p_full,sc_full = improve_full(best_p, full_end)
     update
  # Perturbations
  for sigma in [0.5,1.0]:
     if now() < work_end - 100:
        pe = perturb
        pe,sc = improve_full(pe, min(work_end, now+120))
        update
  # final official
```

But `improve_block_only` is similar to `improve_sequence` without full. `improve_full` does full stages + polish. This separation may allocate well.

Let's write functions:
- `block_refine(p0, end_time, max_B=4096, beta_low=5000, beta_high=30000)`: iterates B list. Does not call full.
- `full_refine(p0, end_time)`: runs B=N stages and maybe a final high beta.

`block_refine`:
```
def block_refine(p0, end_time, max_B=4096):
  p=normalize(p0); sc=fast_score(p)
  B_list = [b for b in BLOCKS if b < p.size and p.size % b ==0 and b <= max_B]
  for B in B_list:
     if now() > end_time - 60: break
     q=orient(p)
     c=c_from_block(q,n,B)
     sub_end=min(end_time, now()+90)
     c=run_block_minimize(c,n,B,5000,sub_end,150)
     q2=block_to_p(...); sc2=fast_score
     if sc2<sc: q=q2; sc=sc2
     if now() < end_time - 45:
        c=c_from_block(q,n,B)
        sub_end=min(end_time, now()+90)
        c=run_block_minimize(c,n,B,30000,sub_end,150)
        q2=...; if sc2<sc update
     p=q
  return normalize(p), sc
```
This may spend up to 180s per B * many = too long. But end_time controls; if end_time 700s, with 8 B and 90s each =720. okay. Use sub_end `min(end_time, now()+60)` for low, `now()+45` high to reduce. Total per B 105, 8=840 >700, time check stops at ~6 B. Good.

`full_refine`:
```
def full_refine(p0,end_time):
  p=normalize; sc=fast_score
  n=p.size
  stages=[(5000,300,250),(20000,400,300),(80000,300,250),(150000,200,200)]
  for beta,maxiter,base_time in stages:
     if now() > end_time - 80: break
     q=orient(p)
     c=c_from_block(q,n,n)
     sub_end=min(end_time - 60, now()+base_time)
     if sub_end <= now()+30: continue
     c=run_block_minimize(c,n,n,beta,sub_end,maxiter)
     q2=block_to_p
     sc2=fast_score
     if sc2<sc: q=q2; sc=sc2
     p=q
  return normalize(p), sc
```
This may use up to 1000s. Good.

Now, Fourier search also can use current best as start? Low-dimensional variables can't start from arbitrary p easily except projecting p onto Fourier subspace. We can include a Fourier init from best p: compute coefficients of log(p) in basis? Since theta can be any; p=exp(theta) normalized. We can set theta0 = log(p) - mean, then project onto Fourier basis: u0 = basis @ theta0? Wait theta = basis.T @ u; least squares u0 = (basis @ theta0) / (norm columns?) Since basis columns not orthonormal. For cos/sin columns over n, norm^2=n/2. If we use `u0 = basis @ theta0 * (2/n)` for each non-DC mode. This projects theta0 onto subspace. Good. Add as an init for each K. This allows Fourier search to continue current structure.

`theta0 = np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); theta0 -= mean; u0 = (2.0/n)* (basis @ theta0)` because columns cos/sin norm squared n/2. But our basis columns not scaled; yes. If n even, sin/cos orthogonal. For k=n/2? K small. This init may be useful. Add to inits after best available? But `fourier_search` runs before block and may use initial top best. We can pass `best_p` to `fourier_search` and include its projection.

In `fourier_search` signature add `seed_p`. For each K, include `u_seed = project_fourier(seed_p,basis,n)`.

This can help if hs already good; Fourier subspace can polish global structure.

theta=theta - mean

return u

`fft_conv`: For n=1, returns [a0^2]. For n=0. Need size next pow2. Use:
```
size=1
while size<need: size <<=1
F=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
b=np.fft.irfft(F*F,size)[:need]
b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
```
Clipping negative may slightly increase max if true negative? For nonnegative conv true nonnegative; negative only roundoff. Good.

`corr_weights`: Similarly roundoff. Use `np.ascontiguousarray`.

Now, test gradient of block objective with B=n and p uniform. c=0; z all 0; wb uniform. p uniform. b triangle. beta low: weights spread. s = corr(p,w). For symmetric w, s uniform? If w symmetric and p uniform, s_i=∑ p_j w_{i+j}. For uniform p, s_i = (1/n)∑_{j=0}^{n-1} w_{i+j} = (window sum of w)/n, which varies near boundaries? Since w length 2n-1 symmetric, window sum length n for i changes? It may not be uniform because w truncated? But for smooth max weights concentrated around center, window sum near center? At uniform, if weights symmetric and full, derivative of convolution max maybe not exactly zero if weights not flat over all positions? Uniform is stationary for exact symmetric max? For smooth max with weights depending on convolution, at uniform all central coefficients equal? The weights around center symmetrical but window sum may be constant for weights restricted to a contiguous block centered? If weights over all positions symmetric, ∑_{j=i}^{i+n-1} w_j may vary with i because weights not constant. Thus uniform may have nonzero gradient under smooth max, causing L-BFGS to move. That's okay. For exact max set of all equal central? At uniform, many coefficients not equal max (only center). If smooth beta low, weights spread to lower coefficients, gradient may leave uniform.

Let's draft code in mind, checking syntax.

```
"""
... docstring
"""
import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY = False

BLOCK_BOUND = 25.0
```

Functions:

`next_pow2(x)`:
```
def next_pow2(x):
    s=1
    while s < x:
        s <<= 1
    return s
```

`fft_conv(a)`:
```
def fft_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0,dtype=np.float64)
    if n==1: return np.array([a[0]*a[0]],dtype=np.float64)
    need=2*n-1
    size=next_pow2(need)
    F=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    b=np.fft.irfft(F*F,size)[:need]
    b=np.ascontiguousarray(b,dtype=np.float64)
    return np.maximum(b,0.0)
```

`fast_score_array(a)`:
```
def fast_score_array(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0: return float('inf')
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    S=float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(S): return float('inf')
    b=fft_conv(a)
    if b.size==0: return float('inf')
    M=float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(M): return float('inf')
    return float(2.0*a.size*M/(S*S))
```

`normalize_simplex(a)`:
```
def normalize_simplex(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0: return a
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       return np.full(a.shape,1.0/float(a.size),dtype=np.float64)
    a *= 1.0/s
    return a
```

`softmax_from_theta(theta)`:
```
def softmax_from_theta(theta):
    theta=np.asarray(theta,dtype=np.float64)
    if theta.size==0: return np.empty(0)
    theta=np.where(np.isfinite(theta),theta,0.0)
    mx=float(np.max(theta))
    e=np.exp(theta-mx)
    s=float(np.sum(e))
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       return np.full(theta.shape,1.0/float(theta.size),dtype=np.float64)
    return e/s
```

`block_to_p(n,B,c)`:
```
def block_to_p(n,B,c):
    if n<=0: return np.empty(0)
    if B<=1: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    if B>n: B=n
    L=n//B
    if L==0: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    z=np.empty(B,dtype=np.float64)
    c=np.asarray(c,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if c.size != B-1:
       tmp=np.zeros(B-1,dtype=np.float64)
       m=min(tmp.size,c.size)
       if m>0: tmp[:m]=c[:m]
       c=tmp
    z[:-1]=c
    z[-1]=0.0
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
       return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    zmax=float(np.max(z))
    e=np.exp(z-zmax)
    s=float(np.sum(e))
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       wb=np.ones(B,dtype=np.float64)/float(B)
    else:
       wb=e/s
    H=wb/float(L)
    p=np.repeat(H,L)
    # fix length if n not multiple? we assume divides; if not, pad/truncate
    if p.size != n:
       p=np.resize(p,n)
       p=normalize_simplex(p)
    return p
```
If n%B !=0, `np.repeat(H,L)` size B*L < n. We can avoid calling non-dividing, but safe pad by repeating last? `np.resize` repeats. But for gradient not. Fine.

`c_from_block(p,n,B)`:
```
def c_from_block(p,n,B):
   if B<=1: return np.empty(0)
   L=n//B
   if L==0: return zeros
   bs=p.reshape(B,L).sum(axis=1)
   bs=np.maximum(bs,1e-300)
   c=np.log(bs[:-1]/bs[-1])
   c=np.where(np.isfinite(c),c,0.0)
   return np.clip(c,-BLOCK_BOUND,BLOCK_BOUND)
```
If n%B !=0, reshape error; avoid.

`smooth_max_weights(vals,beta)`:
```
def smooth_max_weights(vals,beta):
    vals=np.asarray(vals,dtype=np.float64)
    if vals.size==0: return float('inf'), np.empty(0)
    m=float(np.max(vals))
    if not np.isfinite(m): return float('inf'), np.zeros_like(vals)
    if m <= 0.0 or beta <= 0.0:
       w=np.full(vals.size,1.0/float(vals.size))
       return m,w
    x=beta*(vals-m)
    x=np.clip(x,-745.0,0.0)
    e=np.exp(x)
    Z=float(np.sum(e))
    if Z <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(Z):
       w=np.zeros_like(vals,dtype=np.float64)
       w[int(np.argmax(vals))]=1.0
       Z=1.0
    else:
       w=e/Z
    G = m + float(np.log(Z))/beta
    return float(G), w
```

`corr_weights(a,w)` as above:
```
def corr_weights(a,w):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64); w=np.asarray(w,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size; m=w.size
    if n==0 or m==0: return np.zeros(max(n,0),dtype=np.float64)
    need=n+m-1
    size=next_pow2(need)
    P=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    WR=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1].copy(),size)
    C=np.fft.irfft(P*WR,size)[:need]
    s=C[m-n:m][::-1].copy()
    s=np.where(np.isfinite(s),s,0.0)
    return np.maximum(s,0.0)
```
Check slice when m-n can be 0? m=2n-1, n positive, m-n=n-1, okay. For n=1, m=1, C[0:1][::-1].

`block_objective` as above. Need if `L>1` and `g.size != B*L`: due rounding? n exact. Good.
```
def block_objective(c,n,B,beta,deadline):
    if time.monotonic() > deadline:
       return float('inf'), np.zeros(max(0,B-1),dtype=np.float64)
    ...
    val = float(n*G)
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return val, grad.astype(np.float64)
```

`run_block_minimize`:
```
def run_block_minimize(c0,n,B,beta,deadline,maxiter):
    if not HAVE_SCIPY or B<=1:
        return np.asarray(c0,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    c0=np.asarray(c0,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if c0.size != B-1:
       tmp=np.zeros(B-1,dtype=np.float64); ...; c0=tmp
    c0=np.clip(c0,-BLOCK_BOUND,BLOCK_BOUND)
    bounds=[(-BLOCK_BOUND,BLOCK_BOUND)]*(B-1)
    options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':1e-10,'gtol':1e-5,'maxls':40,'maxcor':10}
    try:
       res=minimize(block_objective,c0,args=(n,B,beta,deadline),method='L-BFGS-B',jac=True,bounds=bounds,options=options)
       c=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
       c=c0
    if c.size != B-1:
       c=c0
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
       c=c0
    c=np.clip(c,-BLOCK_BOUND,BLOCK_BOUND)
    return c
```
If deadline already passed before call, run? We check in caller. If `minimize` sees objective inf, may raise.

`project_simplex(v,target=1.0)` maybe not used if no projected gradient. Could skip. But for perturbations? We use softmax. Not needed. But maybe for resampling? no. We can omit to reduce code. But maybe official fallback uniform list. No projection needed.

`orient_sequence(p)`:
```
def orient_sequence(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n=p.size
    if n<10: return p
    k=max(1,n//100)
    if float(np.sum(p[-k:])) < float(np.sum(p[:k])):
       p=p[::-1]
    return p
```

`smooth_random_theta` as above.

`parametric_starts`:
Need ensure all arrays length n. In power law `np.power(np.abs(t), alpha)` at t=0 is 0, okay. If alpha non-integer and t=0, 0^positive=0. Good. `normalize_simplex` if all zero? not.

`random_block_starts`:
```
def random_block_starts(n,rng,B_list,count=2):
    starts=[]
    for B in B_list:
       if B>=n or B<=1 or n%B !=0: continue
       for _ in range(count):
          c=rng.normal(0.0,0.4,size=B-1)
          if c.size>0:
             # lowpass via simple moving average
             kernel=np.ones(3,dtype=np.float64)/3.0
             c=np.convolve(c,kernel,mode='same')
          p=block_to_p(n,B,c)
          starts.append(p)
    return starts
```
`np.convolve` with `mode='same'` returns same length; if B-1=1? okay. If B-1=0? B=1 skip. If c size 2? okay.

`build_fourier_basis(n,K)`:
```
def build_fourier_basis(n,K):
    if K<=0: return np.empty((0,n),dtype=np.float64)
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
    cols=[]
    for k in range(1,K+1):
       ang=2.0*np.pi*float(k)*i/float(n)
       cols.append(np.cos(ang))
       cols.append(np.sin(ang))
    return np.vstack(cols).astype(np.float64)
```

`fourier_objective(u,n,K,beta,deadline,basis)`:
```
def fourier_objective(u,n,K,beta,deadline,basis):
   if time.monotonic() > deadline:
      return float('inf'), np.zeros(2*K,dtype=np.float64)
   u=np.asarray(u,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
   if u.size != 2*K:
       tmp=np.zeros(2*K,dtype=np.float64); m=min(...); tmp[:m]=u[:m]; u=tmp
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
       return inf, zeros
   theta=basis.T @ u
   p=softmax_from_theta(theta)
   b=fft_conv(p)
   G,w=smooth_max_weights(b,beta)
   if not np.isfinite(G): return inf, zeros
   s=corr_weights(p,w)
   ga=2.0*s
   dot=float(np.dot(p,ga))
   gt=p*(ga-dot)
   gu=basis @ gt
   gu=np.where(np.isfinite(gu),gu,0.0)
   return float(n*G), gu.astype(np.float64)
```
Need if basis shape (0,n) K=0: return G uniform? K=0 not used. If K=0, `2*K=0`. no.

`run_fourier_lbfqgs`:
```
def run_fourier_lbfqgs(u0,n,K,basis,beta,deadline,maxiter,limit):
   if not HAVE_SCIPY or K<=0: return u0
   u0=np.asarray(u0,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
   if u0.size != 2*K: ...
   u0=np.clip(u0,-limit,limit)
   bounds=[(-limit,limit)]*(2*K)
   options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':1e-10,'gtol':1e-4,'maxls':30,'maxcor':8}
   try:
      res=minimize(fourier_objective,u0,args=(n,K,beta,deadline,basis),method='L-BFGS-B',jac=True,bounds=bounds,options=options)
      c=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64)
   except Exception:
      c=u0
   if c.size !=2*K: c=u0
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)): c=u0
   c=np.clip(c,-limit,limit)
   return c
```

`project_to_fourier(p,basis,n)`:
```
def project_to_fourier(p,basis,n):
    if basis.size==0: return np.empty(0)
    p=np.maximum(p,1e-300)
    theta=np.log(p)
    theta -= float(np.mean(theta))
    u=basis @ theta
    u = u * (2.0/float(n))
    u=np.where(np.isfinite(u),u,0.0)
    return u
```
This assumes columns orthogonal. Good.

`fourier_search` detailed:
```
def fourier_search(n,K_list,deadline,rng,seed_p):
    best_p = normalize_simplex(seed_p) if seed_p is not None else np.full(n,1.0/n)
    best_score = fast_score_array(best_p)
    beta_stages=[(800,200),(3000,300),(15000,300),(60000,250)]
    remaining_levels=len(K_list)
    for K in K_list:
        if remaining_levels <=0 or now() > deadline - 120: break
        K_budget=(deadline-now())/float(remaining_levels)
        K_end=now()+K_budget
        if K_end <= now()+30: break
        basis=build_fourier_basis(n,K)
        if basis.size == 0:
            continue
        limit = 2.0 if K <= 8 else (1.5 if K <= 16 else 1.0)
        inits=[]
        inits.append(np.zeros(2*K,dtype=np.float64))
        for amp in (0.25,0.6,1.0):
            inits.append(amp*rng.normal(size=2*K))
        if seed_p is not None:
            u_seed=project_to_fourier(best_p,basis,n) # maybe use current best, not seed_p
            inits.append(np.clip(u_seed, -limit,limit))
        # remove duplicates? not needed
        # Each init stages share budget
        total_steps=(len(inits))*len(beta_stages)
        for u0 in inits:
            if now() > K_end - 30: break
            u=u0
            for bs_idx,(beta,maxiter) in enumerate(beta_stages):
                if now() > K_end - 15: break
                steps_left=(len(beta_stages)-bs_idx) + len(inits)*0? Actually per init. Use stage_budget = max(10, (K_end - now())/ max(1, (len(beta_stages)-bs_idx)))? But next inits also. Better not precise; use min(45, (K_end-now())/4)
                stage_budget = max(10.0, (K_end - now()) / max(1, (len(beta_stages)-bs_idx + len(inits)?)))
```

`block_refine`:
```
def block_refine(p0,end_time,max_B=4096):
    p=normalize_simplex(p0)
    n=p.size
    sc=fast_score_array(p)
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return p,sc
    blocks=[1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128,256,512,1024,2048,4096,8192]
    B_list=[b for b in blocks if b<n and b>=2 and b<=max_B and n%b==0]
    B_list.sort()
    for B in B_list:
        if now() > end_time - 60: break
        q=orient_sequence(p)
        c=c_from_block(q,n,B)
        sub_end = min(end_time, now() + 70.0)
        c2 = run_block_minimize(c,n,B,5000.0,sub_end,150)
        q2=block_to_p(n,B,c2)
        sc2=fast_score_array(q2)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < sc:
            q=q2; sc=sc2
        if now() < end_time - 45:
            c=c_from_block(q,n,B)
            sub_end = min(end_time, now() + 50.0)
            c2=run_block_minimize(c,n,B,30000.0,sub_end,150)
            q2=block_to_p(n,B,c2); sc2=fast_score_array(q2)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2<sc:
                q=q2; sc=sc2
        p=normalize_simplex(q)
    return p, sc
```
If `run_block_minimize` with B small and sub_end 70s may do many iter; okay. Might spend too long on small B; but end_time controls. If B_list 8, 70+50=120s each, total 960; if end_time 700, stops after ~5 B. Fine.

`full_refine`:
```
def full_refine(p0,end_time):
   p=normalize_simplex(p0); n=p.size; sc=fast_score
   stages=[(5000,300,240),(20000,400,300),(80000,350,260),(150000,250,220)]
   for beta,maxiter,base_time in stages:
      if now() > end_time - 90: break
      q=orient_sequence(p)
      c=c_from_block(q,n,n)
      sub_end = min(end_time - 60, now() + base_time)
      if sub_end <= now()+30: continue
      c2=run_block_minimize(c,n,n,beta,sub_end,maxiter)
      q2=block_to_p(n,n,c2); sc2=fast_score_array(q2)
      if finite and sc2<sc: q=q2; sc=sc2
      p=normalize_simplex(q)
   return p,sc
```
If n=8192, B=n, c length8191. L-BFGS with maxiter400, sub_end300 maybe heavy. Good.

`perturbed_sequence(p,rng,sigma)`:
```
def perturbed_sequence(p,rng,sigma):
   n=p.size
   d=smooth_random_theta(n,rng,sigma=1.0,cor=24)
   theta=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
   p2=softmax_from_theta(theta + sigma*d)
   return normalize_simplex(p2)
```

`resample_sequence` as above. It should not normalize? maybe return raw; caller normalizes.

`propose_candidate` main:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    try: seed=int(seed)
    except: seed=42
    try: budget_s=float(budget_s)
    except: budget_s=2400.0
    if budget_s <= 0:
       return [1.0]
    start=now()
    reserve = 150.0 if budget_s > 900 else 40.0
    deadline = start + max(60.0, budget_s - reserve)
    # choose N
    if budget_s < 250: N=1024
    elif budget_s < 800: N=4096
    else: N=8192
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    candidates=[]
    # height seq
    hs=None
    try:
       hs = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
       hs = None
    if hs is not None:
       try:
          arr=np.asarray(hs,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
          if arr.size>0:
             hp=normalize_simplex(resample_sequence(arr,N))
             candidates.append(hp)
             candidates.append(hp[::-1])
       except Exception:
          pass
    # param/random
    try:
       candidates.extend(parametric_starts(N,rng))
       candidates.extend(random_block_starts(N,rng,[16,64,256],2))
    except Exception:
       pass
    if not candidates:
       candidates.append(np.full(N,1.0/N))
    # limit candidates maybe 80 to save
    if len(candidates) > 80:
       candidates = candidates[:80]
    # select top
    scored=[]
    for idx,p in enumerate(candidates):
       sc=fast_score_array(p)
       if np.isfinite(sc): scored.append((sc,idx))
    scored.sort()
    top_indices = [idx for sc,idx in scored[:5]]
    if not top_indices:
       best_p=np.full(N,1.0/N); best_score=fast_score_array(best_p)
    else:
       best_p=candidates[top_indices[0]]; best_score=scored[0][0]
       top_ps=[]
       for idx in top_indices:
          top_ps.append(candidates[idx])
    else: top_ps=[best_p]
    print(f"initial N={N} best={best_score:.10f}")
    if best_score > 2.0: # uniform tie? keep uniform maybe
       best_p=np.full(N,1.0/N); best_score=2.0
    # Fourier
    if now() < deadline - 150:
       remaining = deadline - now()
       f_end = now() + min(0.30*remaining, 700.0)
       try:
          p_f,sc_f=fourier_search(N,[4,8,16,32],f_end,rng,best_p)
          if np.isfinite(sc_f) and sc_f < best_score:
             best_p=p_f; best_score=sc_f
              print(f"after fourier {best_score:.10f}")
       except Exception:
          pass
    # Block main
    if now() < deadline - 200:
       remaining=deadline-now()
       b_end=now()+min(0.32*remaining, 700.0)
       try:
          p_b,sc_b=block_refine(best_p,b_end,max_B=2048)
          if np.isfinite(sc_b) and sc_b < best_score:
             best_p=p_b; best_score=sc_b
             print(f"after block main {best_score:.10f}")
       except Exception:
          pass
       # second start
       if len(top_ps)>1 and now() < b_end - 180:
          # choose second with score < best+0.08
          for p2 in top_ps[1:]:
             sc2=fast_score_array(p2)
             if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 <= best_score + 0.05:
                sub_end=min(b_end, now()+300.0)
                try:
                   p2b,sc2b=block_refine(p2,sub_end,max_B=1024)
                   if np.isfinite(sc2b) and sc2b < best_score:
                      best_p=p2b; best_score=sc2b
                      print(f"after block second {best_score:.10f}")
                except Exception: pass
                break
    # Full refine
    if now() < deadline - 180:
       full_end = min(deadline, now() + max(300.0, 0.5*(deadline-now()))) # use most
       # but reserve 120 for perturb/final?
       full_end = min(deadline - 120, now() + max(300.0, 0.55*(deadline-now())))
       try:
          p_full,sc_full=full_refine(best_p,full_end)
          if np.isfinite(sc_full) and sc_full < best_score:
             best_p=p_full; best_score=sc_full
             print(f"after full {best_score:.10f}")
       except Exception: pass
    # Perturbations
    for sigma in (0.7,1.3):
       if now() < deadline - 100:
          try:
             pp=perturbed_sequence(best_p,rng,sigma)
             scpp=fast_score_array(pp)
             pert_end = min(deadline - 20, now() + 150.0)
             pp,scpp=full_refine(pp,pert_end) # full_refine uses stages may be heavy; 150s okay?
             if np.isfinite(scpp) and scpp < best_score:
                best_p=pp; best_score=scpp
                print(f"after perturb {best_score:.10f}")
          except Exception:
             pass
    # finalize
    final=normalize_simplex(best_p)
    final=np.maximum(final,0.0)
    final=np.minimum(final,1000.0)
    s=float(np.sum(final)); if s>0 and finite: final*=1.0/s
    seq=[float(x) for x in final]
    off=official_score(seq)
    if not np.isfinite(off) or off > 2.0 + 1e-8:
       return [1.0] * N
    # If uniform fallback better (unlikely)
    if off > 2.0:
       seq_uniform=[1.0]*N
       offu=official_score(seq_uniform)
       if np.isfinite(offu) and offu < off:
          return seq_uniform
    return seq
```

```python
"""
Search for a nonnegative step-function sequence minimizing

    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2

using several complementary heuristics:

1. A broad candidate pool:
   - the known previous construction if exposed as height_sequence_1,
   - analytic families (cosh, power-law, chirp, sine/cosine, random lowpass),
   - random block starts.

2. Low-dimensional global optimization in Fourier/soft-fmax parameterizations.
   This gives strong global structure and escapes the stationary uniform point.

3. Coarse-to-fine block refinement.  The sequence is represented as piecewise
   constant log-weights, optimized by L-BFGS-B on a smooth-max surrogate.

4. Full-length refinement in the same block-log parameterization.

5. Final perturbation attempts and an authoritative evaluate_sequence check.

All helper functions are top-level and use no lambdas/closures.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY = False

BLOCK_BOUND = 25.0
FULL_BLOCK_TIME = 260.0


def now():
    return time.monotonic()


def next_pow2(x):
    s = 1
    while s < x:
        s <<= 1
    return s


def fft_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([a[0] * a[0]], dtype=np.float64)

    need = 2 * n - 1
    size = next_pow2(need)
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    b = np.fft.irfft(F * F, size)[:need]
    b = np.ascontiguousarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.maximum(b, 0.0)


def normalize_simplex(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(a.shape, 1.0 / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a *= 1.0 / s
    return a


def softmax_from_theta(theta):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    if theta.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = np.where(np.isfinite(theta), theta, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(theta))
    e = np.exp(theta - mx)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(theta.shape, 1.0 / float(theta.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def fast_score_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return float("inf")
    b = fft_conv(a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    M = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * M / (S * S))


def smooth_max_weights(vals, beta):
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=np.float64)
    if vals.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    m = float(np.max(vals))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros_like(vals, dtype=np.float64)

    if m <= 0.0 or beta <= 0.0:
        w = np.full(vals.size, 1.0 / float(vals.size), dtype=np.float64)
        return m, w

    x = beta * (vals - m)
    x = np.clip(x, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    Z = float(np.sum(e))

    if Z <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(Z):
        w = np.zeros_like(vals, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(vals))] = 1.0
        Z = 1.0
    else:
        w = e / Z

    G = m + float(np.log(Z)) / beta
    return float(G), w


def corr_weights(a, w):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    m = w.size
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    need = n + m - 1
    size = next_pow2(need)
    P = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    WR = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1].copy(), size)
    C = np.fft.irfft(P * WR, size)[:need]
    s = C[m - n:m][::-1].copy()
    s = np.where(np.isfinite(s), s, 0.0)
    return np.maximum(s, 0.0)


def block_to_p(n, B, c):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if B <= 1:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if B > n:
        B = n

    L = n // B
    if L == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    z = np.empty(B, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if c.size != B - 1:
        tmp = np.zeros(B - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(tmp.size, c.size)
        if m > 0:
            tmp[:m] = c[:m]
        c = tmp

    z[:-1] = c
    z[-1] = 0.0

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        wb = np.ones(B, dtype=np.float64) / float(B)
    else:
        wb = e / s

    H = wb / float(L)
    p = np.repeat(H, L)
    if p.size != n:
        p = np.resize(p, n)
        p = normalize_simplex(p)
    return p


def c_from_block(p, n, B):
    if B <= 1:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = n // B
    if L == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    bs = p.reshape(B, L).sum(axis=1)
    bs = np.maximum(bs, 1e-300)
    c = np.log(bs[:-1] / bs[-1])
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return np.clip(c, -BLOCK_BOUND, BLOCK_BOUND)


def block_objective(c, n, B, beta, deadline):
    if now() > deadline:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(max(0, B - 1), dtype=np.float64)

    if B <= 1:
        p = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        b = fft_conv(p)
        G, _ = smooth_max_weights(b, beta)
        if not np.isfinite(G):
            return float("inf"), np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
        return float(n * G), np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    z = np.empty(B, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if c.size != B - 1:
        tmp = np.zeros(B - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(tmp.size, c.size)
        if m > 0:
            tmp[:m] = c[:m]
        c = tmp

    z[:-1] = c
    z[-1] = 0.0

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(B - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        wb = np.ones(B, dtype=np.float64) / float(B)
    else:
        wb = e / s

    L = n // B
    if L == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(B - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    p = np.repeat(wb / float(L), L)
    b = fft_conv(p)
    G, w = smooth_max_weights(b, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(G):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(B - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    svec = corr_weights(p, w)
    g = 2.0 * svec

    if g.size != n:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(B - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    if L > 1:
        blockgrad = g.reshape(B, L).sum(axis=1)
    else:
        blockgrad = g

    dot = float(np.dot(wb, blockgrad))
    pref = wb / float(L)
    grad_full = pref * (blockgrad - dot)

    grad = grad_full[:-1]
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return float(n * G), grad.astype(np.float64)


def run_block_minimize(c0, n, B, beta, deadline, maxiter):
    if not HAVE_SCIPY or B <= 1:
        return np.asarray(c0, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)

    c0 = np.asarray(c0, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if c0.size != B - 1:
        tmp = np.zeros(B - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(tmp.size, c0.size)
        if m > 0:
            tmp[:m] = c0[:m]
        c0 = tmp
    c0 = np.clip(c0, -BLOCK_BOUND, BLOCK_BOUND)

    bounds = [(-BLOCK_BOUND, BLOCK_BOUND)] * (B - 1)
    options = {
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "ftol": 1e-10,
        "gtol": 1e-5,
        "maxls": 40,
        "maxcor": 10,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            block_objective,
            c0,
            args=(n, B, beta, deadline),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options=options,
        )
        c = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        c = c0

    if c.size != B - 1:
        c = c0
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        c = c0
    c = np.clip(c, -BLOCK_BOUND, BLOCK_BOUND)
    return c


def build_fourier_basis(n, K):
    if K <= 0:
        return np.empty((0, n), dtype=np.float64)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    cols = []
    for k in range(1, K + 1):
        ang = 2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * i / float(n)
        cols.append(np.cos(ang))
        cols.append(np.sin(ang))
    return np.vstack(cols).astype(np.float64)


def fourier_objective(u, n, K, beta, deadline, basis):
    if now() > deadline:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(2 * K, dtype=np.float64)

    if K <= 0:
        p = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        b = fft_conv(p)
        G, _ = smooth_max_weights(b, beta)
        if not np.isfinite(G):
            return float("inf"), np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
        return float(n * G), np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if u.size != 2 * K:
        tmp = np.zeros(2 * K, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(tmp.size, u.size)
        if m > 0:
            tmp[:m] = u[:m]
        u = tmp

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(2 * K, dtype=np.float64)

    theta = basis.T @ u
    p = softmax_from_theta(theta)

    b = fft_conv(p)
    G, w = smooth_max_weights(b, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(G):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(2 * K, dtype=np.float64)

    s = corr_weights(p, w)
    ga = 2.0 * s
    dot = float(np.dot(p, ga))
    gt = p * (ga - dot)

    gu = basis @ gt
    gu = np.where(np.isfinite(gu), gu, 0.0)
    return float(n * G), gu.astype(np.float64)


def run_fourier_lbfqgs(u0, n, K, basis, beta, deadline, maxiter, limit):
    if not HAVE_SCIPY or K <= 0:
        return np.asarray(u0, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)

    u0 = np.asarray(u0, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if u0.size != 2 * K:
        tmp = np.zeros(2 * K, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(tmp.size, u0.size)
        if m > 0:
            tmp[:m] = u0[:m]
        u0 = tmp
    u0 = np.clip(u0, -limit, limit)

    bounds = [(-limit, limit)] * (2 * K)
    options = {
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "ftol": 1e-10,
        "gtol": 1e-4,
        "maxls": 30,
        "maxcor": 8,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            fourier_objective,
            u0,
            args=(n, K, beta, deadline, basis),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options=options,
        )
        u = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        u = u0

    if u.size != 2 * K:
        u = u0
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
        u = u0
    u = np.clip(u, -limit, limit)
    return u


def project_to_fourier(p, basis, n):
    if basis.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.maximum(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64), 1e-300)
    theta = np.log(p)
    theta -= float(np.mean(theta))
    u = basis @ theta
    u = u * (2.0 / float(n))
    u = np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, 0.0)
    return u.astype(np.float64)


def fourier_search(n, K_list, deadline, rng, seed_p):
    if seed_p is None:
        best_p = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        best_p = normalize_simplex(seed_p)
    best_score = fast_score_array(best_p)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = float("inf")

    beta_stages = [
        (800.0, 180),
        (3000.0, 240),
        (12000.0, 300),
        (50000.0, 260),
    ]

    remaining_levels = len(K_list)
    for K in K_list:
        if remaining_levels <= 0 or now() > deadline - 120.0:
            break

        K_budget = (deadline - now()) / float(remaining_levels)
        K_end = now() + K_budget
        if K_end <= now() + 30.0:
            break
        remaining_levels -= 1

        basis = build_fourier_basis(n, K)
        if basis.size == 0:
            continue

        limit = 2.0 if K <= 8 else (1.5 if K <= 16 else 1.0)

        inits = []
        inits.append(np.zeros(2 * K, dtype=np.float64))
        inits.append(0.5 * rng.normal(size=2 * K).astype(np.float64))
        inits.append(1.0 * rng.normal(size=2 * K).astype(np.float64))
        u_seed = project_to_fourier(best_p, basis, n)
        inits.append(np.clip(u_seed, -limit, limit).astype(np.float64))

        total_stages = len(inits) * len(beta_stages)
        done = 0

        for ii, uinit in enumerate(inits):
            if now() > K_end - 20.0:
                break
            u = np.asarray(uinit, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            for jj, (beta, maxiter) in enumerate(beta_stages):
                if now() > K_end - 10.0:
                    break
                stage_budget = max(8.0, (K_end - now()) / float(max(1, total_stages - done)))
                sub_end = min(K_end, now() + min(stage_budget, K_end - now()))
                if sub_end <= now() + 3.0:
                    break

                u = run_fourier_lbfqgs(u, n, K, basis, beta, sub_end, maxiter, limit)
                p = softmax_from_theta(basis.T @ u)
                sc = fast_score_array(p)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    print(f"  fourier K={K} init={ii+1} beta={beta:.0f} score={best_score:.10f}")
                done += 1

    return best_p, best_score


def smooth_random_theta(n, rng, sigma=1.0, cor=16.0):
    z = np.fft.rfft(rng.standard_normal(n))
    freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
    z = z * np.exp(-((freq * float(n)) / float(cor)) ** 2)
    d = np.fft.irfft(z, n)
    d = d - float(np.mean(d))
    std = float(np.std(d))
    if std <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(std):
        d = rng.standard_normal(n)
        std = float(np.std(d)) + 1e-12
    return (sigma / std) * d


def parametric_starts(n, rng):
    starts = []
    starts.append(np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64))

    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    t = 2.0 * x - 1.0

    for lam in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0):
        p = np.cosh(lam * t)
        starts.append(normalize_simplex(p))

    for alpha in (0.3, 0.7, 1.0, 2.0):
        p = np.power(np.abs(t), alpha)
        starts.append(normalize_simplex(p))

    for r in (0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 0.85):
        i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
        p = np.exp(-r * i) + np.exp(-r * (n - 1.0 - i))
        starts.append(normalize_simplex(p))

    for k in (1, 2, 3, 4, 6):
        for sig in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
            phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            p = np.exp(sig * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * x + phase))
            starts.append(normalize_simplex(p))

    for sig in (0.3, 0.6, 0.9):
        p = 1.0 + sig * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
        starts.append(normalize_simplex(p))

    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    ii = (i * (i + 1.0) * 0.5) / float(n)
    for gamma in (1, 2, 4, 8):
        for sig in (0.5, 0.9):
            p = 1.0 + sig * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(gamma) * ii)
            starts.append(normalize_simplex(p))

    for _ in range(6):
        theta = smooth_random_theta(n, rng, sigma=1.0, cor=16.0)
        p = softmax_from_theta(theta)
        starts.append(p)

    return starts


def random_block_starts(n, rng, B_list, count=2):
    starts = []
    for B in B_list:
        if B <= 1 or B >= n or n % B != 0:
            continue
        for _ in range(count):
            c = rng.normal(0.0, 0.4, size=B - 1).astype(np.float64)
            if c.size > 0:
                kernel = np.ones(3, dtype=np.float64) / 3.0
                c = np.convolve(c, kernel, mode="same")
            p = block_to_p(n, B, c)
            starts.append(p)
    return starts


def resample_sequence(seq, n):
    a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    m = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, a[0], dtype=np.float64)
    if m == n:
        return a

    old = np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, float(m - 1), n, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.interp(new, old, a).astype(np.float64)


def orient_sequence(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = p.size
    if n < 10:
        return p
    k = max(1, n // 100)
    if float(np.sum(p[-k:])) < float(np.sum(p[:k])):
        p = p[::-1]
    return p


def perturbed_sequence(p, rng, sigma):
    n = p.size
    d = smooth_random_theta(n, rng, sigma=1.0, cor=24.0)
    theta = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    p2 = softmax_from_theta(theta + sigma * d)
    return normalize_simplex(p2)


def block_refine(p0, end_time, max_B=2048):
    p = normalize_simplex(p0)
    n = p.size
    sc = fast_score_array(p)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return p, sc

    base_blocks = (1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096, 8192, 16384)
    B_list = [b for b in base_blocks if 2 <= b < n and b <= max_B and n % b == 0]
    B_list.sort()

    for B in B_list:
        if now() > end_time - 60.0:
            break

        q = orient_sequence(p)
        c = c_from_block(q, n, B)

        sub_end = min(end_time, now() + 70.0)
        c2 = run_block_minimize(c, n, B, 5000.0, sub_end, 150)
        q2 = block_to_p(n, B, c2)
        sc2 = fast_score_array(q2)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < sc:
            q = q2
            sc = sc2
            print(f"    block B={B} low score={sc:.10f}")

        if now() < end_time - 45.0:
            c = c_from_block(q, n, B)
            sub_end = min(end_time, now() + 50.0)
            c2 = run_block_minimize(c, n, B, 30000.0, sub_end, 150)
            q2 = block_to_p(n, B, c2)
            sc2 = fast_score_array(q2)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < sc:
                q = q2
                sc = sc2
                print(f"    block B={B} high score={sc:.10f}")

        p = normalize_simplex(q)

    return p, sc


def full_refine(p0, end_time):
    p = normalize_simplex(p0)
    n = p.size
    sc = fast_score_array(p)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return p, sc

    stages = [
        (5000.0, 300, 240.0),
        (20000.0, 400, 300.0),
        (80000.0, 350, 260.0),
        (150000.0, 250, 220.0),
    ]

    for beta, maxiter, base_time in stages:
        if now() > end_time - 90.0:
            break

        q = orient_sequence(p)
        c = c_from_block(q, n, n)
        sub_end = min(end_time - 60.0, now() + base_time)
        if sub_end <= now() + 30.0:
            continue

        c2 = run_block_minimize(c, n, n, beta, sub_end, maxiter)
        q2 = block_to_p(n, n, c2)
        sc2 = fast_score_array(q2)

        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < sc:
            q = q2
            sc = sc2
            print(f"    full beta={beta:.0f} score={sc:.10f}")

        p = normalize_simplex(q)

    return p, sc


def official_score(seq):
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    try:
        val = float(val)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    if np.isfinite(val):
        return val
    return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    try:
        seed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed = 42
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 2400.0

    if budget_s <= 0.0:
        return [1.0]

    start_t = now()
    reserve = 150.0 if budget_s > 900.0 else 40.0
    deadline = start_t + max(60.0, budget_s - reserve)

    if budget_s < 250.0:
        N = 1024
    elif budget_s < 800.0:
        N = 4096
    else:
        N = 8192

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    candidates = []

    # Use the previous known construction if available.
    hs = None
    try:
        hs = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        hs = None

    if hs is not None:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
            if arr.size > 0:
                hp = normalize_simplex(resample_sequence(arr, N))
                candidates.append(hp)
                candidates.append(hp[::-1].copy())
        except Exception:
            pass

    if len(candidates) <= 2:
        try:
            candidates.extend(parametric_starts(N, rng))
            candidates.extend(random_block_starts(N, rng, [16, 64, 256], 2))
        except Exception:
            pass

    if not candidates:
        candidates.append(np.full(N, 1.0 / float(N), dtype=np.float64))

    if len(candidates) > 90:
        candidates = candidates[:90]

    scored = []
    for idx, p in enumerate(candidates):
        sc = fast_score_array(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, idx))
    scored.sort()

    top_indices = [idx for (sc, idx) in scored[:5]]
    top_ps = [candidates[idx] for idx in top_indices]

    if not top_ps:
        best_p = np.full(N, 1.0 / float(N), dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = fast_score_array(best_p)
    else:
        best_p = top_ps[0]
        best_score = fast_score_array(best_p)

    if not np.isfinite(best_score) or best_score > 2.0:
        best_p = np.full(N, 1.0 / float(N), dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = 2.0

    print(f"initial N={N} best={best_score:.10f}")

    # Phase 1: low-dimensional global Fourier search.
    if now() < deadline - 150.0:
        remaining = deadline - now()
        f_end = now() + min(0.30 * remaining, 700.0)
        try:
            p_f, sc_f = fourier_search(N, [4, 8, 16, 32], f_end, rng, best_p)
            if np.isfinite(sc_f) and sc_f < best_score:
                best_p = p_f
                best_score = sc_f
                print(f"after fourier score={best_score:.10f}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Phase 2: coarse-to-fine block refinement.
    if now() < deadline - 200.0:
        remaining = deadline - now()
        b_end = now() + min(0.32 * remaining, 700.0)
        max_block_B = 4096 if budget_s >= 1800.0 else 2048
        try:
            p_b, sc_b = block_refine(best_p, b_end, max_B=max_block_B)
            if np.isfinite(sc_b) and sc_b < best_score:
                best_p = p_b
                best_score = sc_b
                print(f"after block main score={best_score:.10f}")
        except Exception:
            pass

        if len(top_ps) > 1 and now() < b_end - 180.0:
            for p2 in top_ps[1:]:
                sc2 = fast_score_array(p2)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 <= best_score + 0.06:
                    sub_end = min(b_end, now() + 300.0)
                    try:
                        p2b, sc2b = block_refine(p2, sub_end, max_B=1024)
                        if np.isfinite(sc2b) and sc2b < best_score:
                            best_p = p2b
                            best_score = sc2b
                            print(f"after block second score={best_score:.10f}")
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                    break

    # Phase 3: full-length refinement.
    if now() < deadline - 180.0:
        full_end = min(deadline - 120.0, now() + max(300.0, 0.55 * (deadline - now())))
        try:
            p_full, sc_full = full_refine(best_p, full_end)
            if np.isfinite(sc_full) and sc_full < best_score:
                best_p = p_full
                best_score = sc_full
                print(f"after full score={best_score:.10f}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Phase 4: short perturbation refinements.
    for sigma in (0.7, 1.3):
        if now() < deadline - 110.0:
            try:
                pp = perturbed_sequence(best_p, rng, sigma)
                pert_end = min(deadline - 20.0, now() + 140.0)
                pp, scpp = full_refine(pp, pert_end)
                if np.isfinite(scpp) and scpp < best_score:
                    best_p = pp
                    best_score = scpp
                    print(f"after perturb sigma={sigma} score={best_score:.10f}")
            except Exception:
                pass

    final_p = normalize_simplex(best_p)
    final_p = np.maximum(final_p, 0.0)
    final_p = np.minimum(final_p, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(final_p))
    if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
        final_p *= 1.0 / s
    else:
        final_p = np.full(N, 1.0 / float(N), dtype=np.float64)

    final_seq = [float(x) for x in final_p]
    off = official_score(final_seq)

    if not np.isfinite(off) or off > 2.0 + 1e-8:
        return [1.0] * N

    return final_seq
```
